identification code to a person for a restricted area unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is a person to whom a restricted area
  identity card has been issued and the card is active; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is in possession of a document that is
  issued or approved by the operator of the aerodrome in accordance with
  a security measure as authorization for the person to enter or remain
  in the restricted area.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Addition of key

**[[316]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-316 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome may add a key to a restricted area identity card only if it is
possible to cancel or remove the key without damaging or altering any
other elements of the card.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Protection of information

**[[317]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-317 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must not add to or modify a restricted area identity card in
any way that might allow the disclosure to CATSA of information about
the person to whom the card has been issued.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation, withdrawal or retrieval

**[[318]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-318 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must cancel, withdraw or retrieve a key that has been issued
to a person who has been issued a restricted area identity card, or a
combination code or personal identification code that has been assigned
to that person, if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person's restricted area identity card has been
  deactivated; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person no longer requires ongoing access to the
  restricted area in the course of their employment.

#### [Records]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777044 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General requirement

- **[[319]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-319 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome and any person designated by the operator to
  issue restricted area identity cards or keys or to assign combination
  codes or personal identification codes must keep updated records at
  the aerodrome respecting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} restricted area identity cards and keys that have
    been issued;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the names of the persons to whom restricted area
    identity cards or keys have been issued;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the names of the persons to whom combination codes
    or personal identification codes have been assigned;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} blank restricted area identity cards in the
    operator's possession;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} restricted area identity cards that have been
    deactivated;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} keys, combination codes or personal identification
    codes that have been cancelled, withdrawn or retrieved;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} deactivated restricted area identity cards that
    have not been retrieved by the operator;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} restricted area identity cards that have been
    reported as lost or stolen;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} steps taken to retrieve deactivated restricted area
    identity cards;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} compliance with section 307; and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} restricted area identity cards that have been
    destroyed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deactivated cards

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), a record respecting a
  restricted area identity card that has been deactivated must be
  retained for at least one year from the day on which the card was
  deactivated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lost or stolen cards

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A record respecting a restricted area identity card
  that has been reported as lost or stolen must be retained for at least
  one year from the card's expiry date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision to Minister

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the aerodrome must provide the
  Minister with the records on reasonable notice given by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-92, s. 21](#1343989-1344936){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1343989-1344936.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-319-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 319"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Restricted Area Access Control Process]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777064 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of identity verification system

**[[320]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-320 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must implement and maintain a restricted area access control
process that uses the identity verification system.

#### [Control of Access to Restricted Areas]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777067 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unauthorized access prohibition

**[[321]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-321 .sectionLabel}** A person must not enter
or remain in a restricted area unless the person

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is a person to whom a restricted area identity card
  has been issued; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is in possession of a document of entitlement, other
  than a restricted area identity card, for the restricted area.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 20
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-321-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 321"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restricted area identity cards ---
conditions of use

- **[[322]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-322 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person to whom a restricted area identity card has been issued must
  not enter or remain in a restricted area unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} they are acting in the course of their employment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the card is in their possession;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the card is active; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} as applicable, they are in possession of a key that
    has been issued to them for the restricted area, or a combination
    code or personal identification code that has been assigned to them
    for the restricted area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(d) does not apply to crew members.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display of restricted area identity cards

- **[[323]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-323 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person to whom a restricted area identity card has been issued must
  not enter or remain in a restricted area unless they visibly display
  the card on their outer clothing at all times.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display of temporary passes

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person to whom a temporary pass has been issued
  must not enter or remain in a restricted area unless they visibly
  display the pass on their outer clothing at all times.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 25
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-323-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 323"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Oversight

**[[324]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-324 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must ensure that a person is not allowed to enter or remain in
a restricted area at the aerodrome unless the person is in possession of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an active restricted area identity card that has been
  issued to the person; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a document of entitlement, other than a restricted
  area identity card, for the restricted area.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 21
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-324-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 324"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Business Continuity Plans]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777096 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Business continuity plans

- **[[325]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-325 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must develop and maintain a business
  continuity plan that, at a minimum, sets out how the operator will
  re-establish normal operations and comply with section 324 in the
  event that the operator is unable to use its restricted area access
  control process to comply with that section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Implementation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the aerodrome must implement its
  business continuity plan and immediately notify the Minister and CATSA
  if the operator discovers that it is unable to use its restricted area
  access control process to comply with section 324.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of delay

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the aerodrome must immediately notify
  the Minister if the operator discovers that it will be unable, for
  more than 24 hours, to use its restricted area access control process
  to comply with section 324.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ministerial access

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the aerodrome must make its business
  continuity plan available to the Minister on reasonable notice given
  by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-92, s. 22](#1343991-1344940){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1343991-1344940.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-325-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 325"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Database backup

**[[326]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-326 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must regularly back up any database that the operator uses as
part of the identity verification system.

#### [Use of Restricted Area Identity Cards, Keys, Combination Codes and Personal Identification Codes]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777108 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General prohibitions

- **[[327]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-327 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person must not

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} lend or give a restricted area identity card or a
    key that has been issued to them to another person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} use a restricted area identity card or a key that
    has been issued to them to allow access to a restricted area at an
    aerodrome to another person without authorization from the operator
    of the aerodrome;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} intentionally alter or otherwise modify a
    restricted area identity card or a key unless they are the operator
    of an aerodrome or a person designated by the operator;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} use a restricted area identity card or a key that
    has been issued to another person;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} have in their possession, without reasonable
    excuse, a restricted area identity card or a key that has been
    issued to another person;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} use a counterfeit restricted area identity card or
    a counterfeit key; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} make a copy of a restricted area identity card or a
    key.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure or use of codes

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person, other than the operator of an aerodrome or
  a person designated by the operator, must not

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} disclose a combination code or personal
    identification code; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} use another person's combination code or personal
    identification code.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report of loss or theft

- **[[328]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-328 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person to whom a restricted area identity card or a key has been
  issued must immediately report its loss or theft to their employer or
  to the operator of an aerodrome who issued the card or key.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Employer's duty to report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An employer who is informed by an employee of the
  loss or theft of a restricted area identity card or a key must
  immediately report the loss or theft to the operator of an aerodrome
  who issued the card or key.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report of non-functioning card

**[[329]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-329 .sectionLabel}** An employer who is
informed by an employee that a restricted area identity card is not
functioning must immediately notify the operator of an aerodrome who
issued the card.

**[[330]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-330 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2014-153, s. 26\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-330-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 330"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Presentation and Surrender of Restricted Area Identity Cards]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777131 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation on demand

- **[[331]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-331 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person in possession of a restricted area identity card who is in a
  restricted area at an aerodrome must, on demand, present the card to
  the Minister, the operator of the aerodrome, the person's employer or
  a peace officer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation during screening

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person in possession of a restricted area identity
  card who is being screened by a screening officer at a restricted area
  access point or at a location in a restricted area must, on demand,
  present the card to the screening officer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Surrender on demand

- **[[332]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-332 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person in possession of a restricted area identity card must, on
  demand, surrender it to the Minister, the operator of an aerodrome, a
  screening officer or a peace officer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Demand by Minister or operator

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister or the operator of an aerodrome may
  demand the surrender of a restricted area identity card if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the card has expired or has been reported as lost
    or stolen;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the card has been deactivated; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the surrender of the card is required to ensure
    aviation security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Demand by screening officer

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A screening officer may demand the surrender of a
  restricted area identity card if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the card has expired or has been reported as lost
    or stolen;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the card has been deactivated; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the screening officer is carrying out screening at
    a restricted area access point or at a location in a restricted area
    and the person who is in possession of the card refuses to be
    screened or refuses to submit goods in their possession or control
    for screening.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Demand by peace officer

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A peace officer may demand the surrender of a
  restricted area identity card if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the card has expired or has been reported as lost
    or stolen; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} there is an immediate threat to aviation security,
    the security of any aircraft or aerodrome or other aviation facility
    or the safety of the public, passengers or crew members, and the
    surrender of the card is required to respond to the threat.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of cards

**[[333]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-333 .sectionLabel}** A screening officer or
a peace officer to whom a person surrenders a restricted area identity
card must return the card to the operator of the aerodrome where the
card is surrendered or to the operator of an aerodrome who issued the
card.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of Minister

**[[334]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-334 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome to whom a person surrenders a restricted area identity card
must notify the Minister if the operator demanded the surrender in
accordance with paragraph 332(2)(c).

#### [Escort and Surveillance]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777158 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General requirement

- **[[335]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-335 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must ensure that any person who is in a
  restricted area at the aerodrome and is not in possession of a
  restricted area identity card

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is escorted by a person in possession of an active
    restricted area identity card that has been issued to them; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is kept under surveillance by a person in
    possession of an active restricted area identity card that has been
    issued to them, in the case of an area the limits of which are
    defined for a specific purpose, such as construction or maintenance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply in respect of the
  following persons:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} passengers who have been screened; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} inspectors.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Escort ratio

- **[[336]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-336 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must ensure that at least one escort is
  provided for every 10 persons who require escort.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Surveillance ratio

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator of an aerodrome must ensure that no more
  than 20 persons at a time are kept under surveillance by one person.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to remain together

- **[[337]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person under escort must remain with the escort while the person is in
  a restricted area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An escort must remain with the person under escort
  while the person is in a restricted area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to inform

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The person who appoints an escort must inform the
  escort of the requirement to remain with the person under escort while
  that person is in a restricted area.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Screening requirement

**[[338]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-338 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must ensure that a person under escort or surveillance at the
aerodrome and any goods in the person's possession or control are
screened at a screening checkpoint before the person enters a sterile
area.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- conveyances

- **[[339]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-339 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome is not required to place an escort or
  surveillance personnel in a conveyance that is in a restricted area at
  the aerodrome and is carrying persons who require escort or
  surveillance if the conveyance travels in a convoy with an escort
  conveyance that contains at least one person in possession of an
  active restricted area identity card that has been issued to them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the aerodrome must ensure that, if a
  person who requires escort or surveillance disembarks from a
  conveyance in a restricted area at the aerodrome, the person is
  escorted or kept under surveillance in accordance with section 336.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Escort conveyances

**[[340]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-340 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must ensure that, at the aerodrome, at least one escort
conveyance is provided for

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} every three conveyances requiring escort to or from
  an air terminal building apron area for a purpose other than snow
  removal operations;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} every six conveyances requiring escort to or from an
  air terminal building apron area for snow removal operations; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} every six conveyances requiring escort to or from a
  restricted area other than an air terminal building apron area.

#### [Inspectors]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777192 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

**[[341]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-341 .sectionLabel}** Nothing in this
Division requires an inspector acting in the course of their employment
to be in possession of a restricted area identity card or any other
document issued or approved by the operator of an aerodrome as
authorization for the inspector to enter or remain in a restricted area.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspector's credentials

**[[342]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-342 .sectionLabel}** The credentials issued
by the Minister to an inspector do not constitute a restricted area
identity card even if the credentials are compatible with the identity
verification system or with an access control system established by the
operator of an aerodrome.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Escort privileges

**[[343]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-343 .sectionLabel}** Nothing in this
Division prohibits an inspector from escorting a person who is in a
restricted area and is not in possession of a restricted area identity
card if the inspector

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is acting in the course of their employment;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} does not escort more than 10 persons at one time;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} remains with the person while the person is in the
  restricted area;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} ensures that the person remains with the inspector
  while the person is in the restricted area; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} ensures that the person and any goods in their
  possession or control are screened at a screening checkpoint before
  the person enters a sterile area.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conveyance escort privileges

- **[[344]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-344
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in this Division prohibits
  an inspector from escorting a person who is in a conveyance in a
  restricted area and is not in possession of a restricted area identity
  card if the inspector

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is acting in the course of their employment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} does not escort more than 10 persons at one time;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is either in the conveyance or in an escort
    conveyance that is travelling in a convoy with the conveyance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a person under escort disembarks from a conveyance
  in a restricted area, the inspector must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} remain with the person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the person remains with the inspector.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a person under escort is travelling to or from an
  air terminal building apron area, the Minister must ensure that at
  least one escort conveyance is provided for every three conveyances
  requiring escort in a convoy and that at least one inspector is in
  each escort conveyance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a person under escort is travelling to or from a
  restricted area other than an air terminal building apron area, the
  Minister must ensure that at least one escort conveyance is provided
  for every six conveyances requiring escort in a convoy and that at
  least one inspector is in each escort conveyance.

### [DIVISION 9]{.HLabel2}[Airport Security Programs]{.HTitleText2} {#h-777218 .Subheading}

#### [Overview]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777219 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Division overview

**[[345]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-345 .sectionLabel}** This Division sets out
the regulatory framework for promoting a comprehensive, coordinated and
integrated approach to airport security. The processes required under
this Division are intended to facilitate the establishment and
implementation of effective airport security programs that reflect the
circumstances of each aerodrome.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-345-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 345"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777224 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Processes and procedures

**[[346]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-346 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
any reference to a process in this Division includes the procedures, if
any, that are necessary to implement that process.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-346-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 346"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Airport Security Program Requirements]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777229 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to establish and implement

- **[[347]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-347 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must establish and implement an airport
  security program.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Program requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} As part of its airport security program, the operator
  of an aerodrome must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} define and document the aerodrome-related security
    roles and responsibilities assigned to each of the operator's
    employee groups and contractor groups;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} communicate the information referred to in
    paragraph (a) to the employees and contractors in those groups;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} have a security policy statement that establishes
    an overall commitment and direction for aerodrome security and sets
    out the operator's security objectives;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} communicate the security policy statement in an
    accessible manner to all persons who are employed at the aerodrome
    or who require access to the aerodrome in the course of their
    employment;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} establish and implement a process for responding to
    aerodrome-related security incidents and breaches in a coordinated
    manner that is intended to minimize their impact;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} establish and implement a security awareness
    program that promotes a culture of security vigilance and awareness
    among the following persons:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} persons who are employed at the aerodrome,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} crew members who are based at the aerodrome, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} persons, other than crew members, who require
      access to the aerodrome in the course of their employment;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} assess risk information and disseminate it within
    the operator's organization for the purpose of informed
    decision-making about aviation security;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} establish and implement a process for receiving,
    retaining, disclosing and disposing of sensitive information
    respecting aviation security in order to protect the information
    from unauthorized access;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} identify sensitive information respecting aviation
    security and receive, retain, disclose and dispose of sensitive
    information respecting aviation security in a manner that protects
    the information from unauthorized access;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} disclose sensitive information respecting aviation
    security to the following persons if they have been assigned
    aerodrome-related security roles and responsibilities and require
    the information to carry out those roles and responsibilities:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} persons who are employed at the aerodrome, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} persons who require access to the aerodrome in
      the course of their employment;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} have a current scale map of the aerodrome that
    identifies all restricted areas, security barriers and restricted
    area access points; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} document how the operator achieves compliance with
    the aviation security provisions of the Act and the regulatory
    requirements that apply to the operator.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other program requirements

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The following also form part of the airport security
  program:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the security official referred to in section 270;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the aerodrome security personnel training referred
    to in sections 271 and 272;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the security committee or other working group or
    forum referred to in section 350;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the multi-agency advisory committee
    referred to in section 353;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the airport security risk assessment
    referred to in section 354;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the strategic airport security plan
    referred to in section 359;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the menu of additional safeguards referred to in
    section 365;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the emergency plan referred to in section 367; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the security exercises referred to in sections 368
    and 369.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, ss. 22, 65(F)
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-347-20120315.html "Link to previous version of section 347"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documentation

- **[[348]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-348 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} keep documentation related to its menu of
    additional safeguards and any amendment to it for at least five
    years;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, keep documentation related to its
    airport security risk assessment and any review of it for at least
    five years;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, keep documentation related to its
    strategic airport security plan and any amendment to it for at least
    five years; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} keep all other documentation related to its airport
    security program for at least two years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ministerial access

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the aerodrome must make the
  documentation available to the Minister on reasonable notice given by
  the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-348-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 348"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to amend

**[[349]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-349 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must amend its airport security program if the operator
identifies, at the aerodrome, an aviation security risk that is not
addressed by the program.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-349-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 349"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Security Committee]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777280 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security committee

- **[[350]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-350 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must have a security committee or other
  working group or forum that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} advises the operator on the development of controls
    and processes that are necessary at the aerodrome in order to comply
    with the aviation security provisions of the Act and the regulatory
    requirements that apply to the operator;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} helps coordinate the implementation of the controls
    and processes that are necessary at the aerodrome in order to comply
    with the aviation security provisions of the Act and the regulatory
    requirements that apply to the operator; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} promotes the sharing of information respecting the
    airport security program.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms of reference

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the aerodrome must manage the
  security committee or other working group or forum in accordance with
  written terms of reference that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} identify its membership; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} define the roles and responsibilities of each
    member.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the aerodrome must keep records of
  the activities and decisions of the security committee or other
  working group or forum.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-350-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 350"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Requirements that Apply only if an Amendment to Schedule 2 or a Ministerial Order is made: Multi-agency Advisory Committee; Airport Security Risk Assessments; and Strategic Airport Security Plans]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777295 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[351]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-351
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 352, sections 353
  to 364 apply to the operator of an aerodrome if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Governor in Council makes an aviation security
    regulation adding an asterisk in Schedule 2 after the International
    Civil Aviation Organization (ICAO) location indicator of the
    aerodrome; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister makes an order stating that
    sections 353 to 364 apply to the operator.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's authority

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister is authorized to make orders stating
  that sections 353 to 364 apply to operators of aerodromes listed in
  Schedule 2.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
- [SOR/2022-92, s. 39](#1344031-1345068){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1344031-1345068.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-351-20140613.html "Link to previous version of section 351"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transition

- **[[352]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-352
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Sections 353 and 356 do not apply to
  the operator of an aerodrome until the day that is six months after
  the earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which an aviation security regulation
    adding an asterisk in Schedule 2 after the International Civil
    Aviation Organization (ICAO) location indicator of the aerodrome
    comes into force, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which a ministerial order stating that
    sections 353 to 364 apply to the operator comes into force.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Sections 354 and 355 do not apply to the operator of
  an aerodrome until the day that is 10 months after the earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which an aviation security regulation
    adding an asterisk in Schedule 2 after the International Civil
    Aviation Organization (ICAO) location indicator of the aerodrome
    comes into force, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which a ministerial order stating that
    sections 353 to 364 apply to the operator comes into force.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transition

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Sections 359 and 361 do not apply to the operator of
  an aerodrome until the day that is 22 months after the earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which an aviation security regulation
    adding an asterisk in Schedule 2 after the International Civil
    Aviation Organization (ICAO) location indicator of the aerodrome
    comes into force, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which a ministerial order stating that
    sections 353 to 364 apply to the operator comes into force.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
- [SOR/2022-92, s. 39](#1344031-1345072){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1344031-1345072.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-352-20140613.html "Link to previous version of section 352"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Multi-agency advisory committee

- **[[353]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-353 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must have a multi-agency advisory committee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Membership

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the aerodrome must invite at least
  the following persons and organizations to be members of the
  multi-agency advisory committee:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Department of Transport;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} CATSA;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the police service with jurisdiction at the
    aerodrome;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the Royal Canadian Mounted Police;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the Canadian Security Intelligence Service; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the Canada Border Services Agency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms of reference

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the aerodrome must manage the
  multi-agency advisory committee in accordance with written terms of
  reference.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objectives

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The objectives of the multi-agency advisory committee
  are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to advise the operator of the aerodrome on its
    airport security risk assessment and its strategic airport security
    plan; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to promote the sharing of sensitive information
    respecting aviation security at the aerodrome.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the aerodrome must keep records of
  the activities and decisions of the multi-agency advisory committee.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Airport security risk assessments

**[[354]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-354 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must have an airport security risk assessment that identifies,
assesses and prioritizes aviation security risks and that includes the
following elements:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a threat assessment that evaluates the probability
  that aviation security incidents will occur at the aerodrome;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a criticality assessment that prioritizes the areas,
  assets, infrastructure and operations at or associated with the
  aerodrome that most require protection from acts and attempted acts of
  unlawful interference with civil aviation;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a vulnerability assessment that considers the extent
  to which the areas, assets, infrastructure and operations at or
  associated with the aerodrome are susceptible to loss or damage and
  that evaluates this susceptibility in the context of the threat
  assessment; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} an impact assessment that, at a minimum, measures the
  consequences of an aviation security incident or potential aviation
  security incident in terms of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a decrease in public safety and security,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} financial and economic loss, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a loss of public confidence.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission for approval

**[[355]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-355 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must submit its airport security risk assessment to the
Minister for approval, and must submit a new airport security risk
assessment to the Minister within five years after the date of the most
recent approval.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to consult

**[[356]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-356 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must consult its multi-agency advisory committee when the
operator is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} preparing its airport security risk assessment for
  submission to the Minister for approval; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} conducting a review of its airport security risk
  assessment.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Airport security risk assessment ---
annual review

- **[[357]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-357 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must conduct a review of its airport security
  risk assessment at least once a year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Airport security risk assessment ---
  other reviews

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the aerodrome must also conduct a
  review of its airport security risk assessment if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a special event that is scheduled to take place at
    the aerodrome could affect aerodrome security;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operator is planning a change to the physical
    layout or operation of the aerodrome that could affect aviation
    security at the aerodrome;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an environmental or operational change at the
    aerodrome could affect aerodrome security;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a change in regulatory requirements could affect
    aerodrome security;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the operator identifies, at the aerodrome, a
    vulnerability that is not addressed in the assessment, or the
    Minister identifies such a vulnerability to the operator; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the Minister informs the operator that there is a
    change in the threat environment that could result in a new or
    unaddressed medium to high risk.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equivalency

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a review conducted under
  subsection (2) counts as a review required under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documentation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} When the operator of the aerodrome conducts a review
  of its airport security risk assessment, the operator must document

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any decision to amend or to not amend the
    assessment or the operator's risk-management strategy;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the reasons for that decision; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the factors that were taken into consideration in
    making that decision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the aerodrome must notify the
  Minister if, as a result of a review of its airport security risk
  assessment, the operator amends the assessment

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to include a new medium to high risk; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to raise or lower the level of a risk within the
    medium to high range.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

**[[358]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-358 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must
approve an airport security risk assessment submitted by the operator of
an aerodrome if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the assessment meets the requirements of section 354;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the assessment has been reviewed by an executive
  within the operator's organization who is responsible for security;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the operator has considered risk information provided
  by its multi-agency advisory committee;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the operator has considered all available and
  relevant information; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the operator has not overlooked an aviation security
  risk that could affect the operation of the aerodrome.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Strategic airport security plans

**[[359]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-359 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must establish a strategic airport security plan that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} summarizes the operator's strategy to prepare for,
  detect, prevent, respond to and recover from acts or attempted acts of
  unlawful interference with civil aviation; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} includes a risk-management strategy that addresses
  the medium to high aviation security risks identified and prioritized
  in the operator's airport security risk assessment.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to consult

**[[360]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-360 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must consult its multi-agency advisory committee when the
operator

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} establishes its strategic airport security plan; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} amends its strategic airport security plan under
  subsection 364(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to submit

**[[361]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must submit its strategic airport security plan to the
Minister for approval.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to implement

**[[362]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-362 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must, as soon as its strategic airport security plan is
approved, implement its risk-management strategy.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval of plan

**[[363]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-363 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must
approve a strategic airport security plan submitted by the operator of
an aerodrome if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the plan meets the requirements of section 359;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the plan has been reviewed by an executive within the
  operator's organization who is responsible for security;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the plan is likely to enable the operator to prepare
  for, detect, prevent, respond to and recover from acts or attempted
  acts of unlawful interference with civil aviation;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the risk-management strategy is in proportion to the
  risks it addresses;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the operator has considered the advice of its
  multi-agency advisory committee;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the operator has not overlooked an aviation security
  risk that could affect the operation of the aerodrome; and

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the plan can be implemented without compromising
  aviation security.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendments

- **[[364]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-364 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome may amend its strategic airport security plan
  at any time, but must do so if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the plan does not reflect the operator's airport
    security risk assessment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister informs the operator that there is a
    change in the threat environment that could result in a new or
    unaddressed medium to high risk;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister informs the operator that its
    risk-management strategy is not in proportion to a medium to high
    risk set out in the operator's airport security risk assessment; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the operator identifies a deficiency in the plan.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documentation --- risk-management
  strategy

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the operator of the aerodrome amends its
  risk-management strategy, the operator must document

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reason for the amendment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the factors that were taken into consideration in
    making that amendment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documentation --- strategic airport
  security plan

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the operator of the aerodrome amends its strategic
  airport security plan, the operator must document

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reason for the amendment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the factors that were taken into consideration in
    making that amendment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission of amendment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the operator of the aerodrome amends its strategic
  airport security plan, the operator must, as soon as possible, submit
  the amendment to the Minister for approval.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must approve an amendment if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an amendment to the summary required
    under paragraph 359(a), the conditions set out in paragraphs 363(a)
    to (c) have been met; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an amendment to the risk-management
    strategy required under paragraph 359(b), the conditions set out in
    section 363 have been met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Implementation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the operator of the aerodrome amends its
  risk-management strategy, the operator must implement the amended
  version of the strategy once it is approved by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

#### [Menu of Additional Safeguards]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777451 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to establish

- **[[365]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-365 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must establish a menu of additional
  safeguards that are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} intended to mitigate heightened risk conditions in
    a graduated manner; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} consistent with the operator's legal powers and
    obligations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Menu requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The menu of additional safeguards must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} describe, by activity type and location, the
    safeguards in place at the aerodrome in respect of AVSEC level 1
    operating conditions;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} allow the rapid selection of additional safeguards
    by activity type or location; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} indicate the persons and organizations responsible
    for implementing each additional safeguard.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Activity types

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraphs (2)(a) and (b), the
  activity types must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} access controls;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} monitoring and patrolling;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} communications; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} other operational controls.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Locations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraphs (2)(a) and (b), the
  locations must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} public areas of the aerodrome;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} areas of the aerodrome that are not public areas
    but are not restricted areas; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} restricted areas.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to submit

**[[366]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-366 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must submit its menu of additional safeguards to the Minister
for approval.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

**[[366.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-366.1 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must
approve a menu of additional safeguards submitted by the operator of an
aerodrome if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the menu meets the requirements of section 365;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the menu has been reviewed by an executive within the
  operator's organization who is responsible for security;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the additional safeguards can be implemented rapidly
  and consistently;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the additional safeguards are consistent with
  existing rights and freedoms; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the additional safeguards can be implemented without
  compromising aviation security.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendments

- **[[366.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-366.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The operator of an aerodrome may
  amend its menu of additional safeguards at any time, but must do so if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister informs the operator that there is a
    change in the threat environment that requires the addition or
    deletion of additional safeguards;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operator identifies a deficiency in the menu;
    or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a change is made in the aviation security
    provisions of the Act or in regulatory requirements and the change
    affects the additional safeguards.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to consult

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If applicable, the operator of the aerodrome must
  consult its multi-agency advisory committee when amending its menu of
  additional safeguards.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission of amendment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the operator of the aerodrome amends its menu of
  additional safeguards, the operator must, as soon as possible, submit
  the amendment to the Minister for approval.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must approve the amendment if the
  conditions set out in section 366.1 continue to be met.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

#### [Emergency Plans]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777502 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Plan requirements

- **[[367]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-367 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must establish an emergency plan that sets
  out the response procedures to be followed at the aerodrome for
  coordinated responses to the following emergencies:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} bomb threats;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} hijackings of aircraft; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} other acts of unlawful interference with civil
    aviation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Response procedures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The response procedures must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} set out in detail the actions to be taken by the
    employees and contractors of the operator of the aerodrome and
    identify the responsibilities of all other persons or organizations
    involved, including, as applicable, the police, emergency response
    providers, air carriers, emergency coordination centre personnel and
    control tower or flight service station personnel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} include detailed procedures for the evacuation of
    air terminal buildings;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} include detailed procedures for the search of air
    terminal buildings;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} include detailed procedures for the handling and
    disposal of a suspected bomb; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} include detailed procedures for the detention on
    the ground of any aircraft involved in a bomb threat or hijacking.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 23
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-367-20120315.html "Link to previous version of section 367"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Security Exercises]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777518 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Operations-based security exercise

- **[[368]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-368 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must, at least once every two years, carry
  out an operations-based security exercise that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} tests the effectiveness of the operator's emergency
    plan in response to an act of unlawful interference with civil
    aviation and involves the persons and organizations referred to in
    the plan; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} tests the effectiveness of additional safeguards
    that the operator chooses from its menu of additional safeguards.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equivalency

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, in response to an aviation security incident, the
  Minister raises the AVSEC level for an aerodrome or any part of an
  aerodrome, the implementation of additional safeguards by the operator
  of the aerodrome counts as an operations-based security exercise for
  the purposes of subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 23
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-368-20120315.html "Link to previous version of section 368"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discussion-based security exercise

- **[[369]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-369 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must, at least once a year, carry out a
  discussion-based security exercise that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} tests the effectiveness of the operator's emergency
    plan in response to an act of unlawful interference with civil
    aviation and involves the persons and organizations referred to in
    the plan; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} tests the effectiveness of additional safeguards
    that the operator chooses from its menu of additional safeguards.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), the operator of an aerodrome
  is not required to carry out a discussion-based security exercise in
  any year in which it carries out an operations-based security
  exercise.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 23
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-369-20120315.html "Link to previous version of section 369"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

**[[370]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-370 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must give the Minister 60 days' notice of any security
exercise that the operator plans to carry out.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

#### [Records]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777541 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional safeguards

- **[[371]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-371 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  time additional safeguards are implemented at an aerodrome in order to
  mitigate heightened risk conditions related to aviation security, the
  operator of the aerodrome must create a record that includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the additional safeguards that
    were implemented;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an evaluation of the effectiveness of those
    additional safeguards; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of any actions that are planned in
    order to address deficiencies identified during the implementation
    of those additional safeguards.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Emergencies

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each time an emergency referred to in
  subsection 367(1) occurs at an aerodrome, the operator of the
  aerodrome must create a record that includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the emergency;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an evaluation of the effectiveness of the
    operator's emergency plan; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of any actions that are planned in
    order to address deficiencies identified during the emergency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exercises

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Each time a security exercise is carried out at an
  aerodrome, the operator of the aerodrome must create a record that
  includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an outline of the exercise scenario;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an evaluation of the effectiveness of the exercise;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of any actions that are planned in
    order to address deficiencies identified during the exercise.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 23
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-371-20120315.html "Link to previous version of section 371"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Corrective Actions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777560 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corrective actions

**[[372]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-372 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 373,
the operator of an aerodrome must immediately take corrective actions to
address a vulnerability that contributes to a heightened aviation
security risk at the aerodrome and that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is identified to the operator by the Minister; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is identified by the operator.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-372-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 372"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corrective action plan

**[[373]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-373 .sectionLabel}** If a corrective action
to be taken by the operator of an aerodrome under section 372 involves a
phased approach, the operator must include in its airport security
program a corrective action plan that sets out

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the nature of the vulnerability to be addressed;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a rationale for the phased approach; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a timetable setting out when each phase of the
  corrective action plan will be completed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-373-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 373"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Primary Security Line Partners]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777574 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of information to operator of
aerodrome

- **[[374]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-374 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purpose of supporting the establishment and implementation of an
  airport security program by the operator of an aerodrome, a primary
  security line partner at the aerodrome must, on reasonable notice
  given by the operator, provide the operator with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} information respecting the measures, procedures and
    processes that the partner has in place at the aerodrome to protect
    the security of restricted areas and to prevent breaches of the
    primary security line; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a document that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} describes each area on the aerodrome's primary
      security line that is occupied by the partner,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} indicates the location of each restricted area
      access point in those areas, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} describes those restricted area access points.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of information to Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The primary security line partner must provide the
  Minister with the information and the document on reasonable notice
  given by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-374-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 374"){.PITLink}
:::

**\[375 to 379 reserved\]**

#### [Disclosure of Information]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777588 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

**[[380]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-380 .sectionLabel}** A person other than the
Minister must not disclose security-sensitive information that is
created or used under this Division unless the disclosure is required by
law or is necessary to comply or facilitate compliance with the aviation
security provisions of the Act, regulatory requirements or the
requirements of an emergency direction.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-380-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 380"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 10]{.HLabel2}[Reserved]{.HTitleText2} {#h-777593 .Subheading}

**\[381 to 390 reserved\]**

### [DIVISION 11]{.HLabel2}[Other Aerodrome Operations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-777595 .Subheading}

#### [Overview]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777596 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Division overview

**[[391]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-391 .sectionLabel}** This Division sets out
requirements respecting aerodrome operations that are not dealt with in
any other Division of this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 24
:::

#### [Construction Plans]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777601 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to notify Minister

- **[[392]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-392 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must notify the Minister of all plans to
  begin new construction or to make a change to the physical security of
  the aerodrome, if the construction or change relates to regulatory
  requirements respecting passengers, aircraft, baggage, cargo or mail.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be in writing;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} state the date on which the construction will begin
    or the change will be made; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} set out a description of the construction or change
    and the safeguards that will be implemented to maintain security in
    the areas of the aerodrome that will be affected by the construction
    activities.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 24
:::

**\[393 to 400 reserved\]**

## [PART 6]{.HLabel1}[Class 3 Aerodromes]{.HTitleText1} {#h-777613 .Part}

### [Overview]{.HTitleText2} {#h-777614 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part overview

**[[401]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-401 .sectionLabel}** This Part sets out the
basic regulatory framework for security at aerodromes listed in Schedule
3 and at any other place designated by the Minister under subsection
6(1.1) of the [Canadian Air Transport Security Authority
Act](/eng/acts/C-11.2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-196, s. 4
- [SOR/2022-92, s. 38](#1344024-1345048){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1344024-1345048.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-401-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 401"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-777619 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[402]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-402 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies in
respect of aerodromes listed in Schedule 3 and in respect of any other
place designated by the Minister under subsection 6(1.1) of the
[Canadian Air Transport Security Authority Act](/eng/acts/C-11.2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-196, s. 5
- [SOR/2022-92, s. 38](#1344024-1345051){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1344024-1345051.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-402-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 402"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Prohibited Items]{.HTitleText2} {#h-777624 .Subheading}

#### [Overview]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777625 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Division overview

**[[403]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-403 .sectionLabel}** This Division completes
and supplements the regulatory framework set out in Part 3.

#### [Authorization for Carriage of or Access to Explosive Substances and Incendiary Devices]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777628 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization

**[[404]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-404 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome may authorize a person to carry or have access to an explosive
substance or an incendiary device at the aerodrome if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the explosive substance or incendiary device is to be
  used at the aerodrome

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for excavation, demolition or construction work,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in fireworks displays,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} by persons operating explosives detection
    equipment or handling explosives detection dogs,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} by a police service, or

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} by military personnel; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operator has reasonable grounds to believe that
  the safety of the aerodrome and the safety of persons and aircraft at
  the aerodrome will not be jeopardized by the presence of the explosive
  substance or incendiary device.

#### [Availability of Prohibited Items]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777638 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- sterile area

- **[[405]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-405 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must not permit goods listed or described in
  the general list of prohibited items or, as applicable, the specific
  list of prohibited items to be made available to persons in a sterile
  area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- liquids, aerosols and
  gels

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of liquids,
  aerosols and gels that are made available to persons in accordance
  with a security measure.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- knives

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of rounded,
  dull-blade knives and plastic knives that are made available to the
  customers of a concessionaire with the permission of the operator of
  the aerodrome.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 25
:::

**\[406 reserved\]**

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Threats and Incidents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-777649 .Subheading}

#### [Overview]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777650 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Division overview

**[[407]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-407 .sectionLabel}** This Division sets out
the regulatory framework for dealing with threats and incidents at an
aerodrome.

#### [Threat Response]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777653 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Area under operator's control

**[[408]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-408 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome who is made aware of a threat against an aviation facility, or
a part of the aerodrome, that is under the operator's control must
immediately determine whether there is a threat that jeopardizes the
security of the facility or that part of the aerodrome.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137855){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137855.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-408-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 408"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Area under control of other person

**[[409]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-409 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome who is made aware of a threat against an aviation facility, or
a part of the aerodrome, that is under the control of a person carrying
on any activity at the aerodrome, other than the operator, must
immediately

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} notify the person of the nature of the threat; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} determine whether there is a threat that jeopardizes
  the security of the aerodrome.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137858){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137858.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-409-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 409"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Threats

**[[410]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-410 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome who determines that there is a threat that jeopardizes the
security of the aerodrome must immediately take all of the measures
necessary to ensure the safety of the aerodrome and persons at the
aerodrome, including informing the appropriate police service of the
nature of the threat.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137862){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137862.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-410-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 410"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties of other person

**[[411]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-411 .sectionLabel}** A person, other than a
screening authority, who is carrying on any activity at an aerodrome and
who is made aware of a threat against the aerodrome must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} immediately notify the operator of the aerodrome of
  the nature of the threat; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} assist the operator of the aerodrome in determining
  whether there is a threat that jeopardizes the security of the
  aerodrome.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137865){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137865.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-411-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 411"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Threats identified by other person

**[[412]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-412 .sectionLabel}** If it is determined
under paragraph 15(b), 409(b) or 411(b) that there is a threat that
jeopardizes the security of an aerodrome, the operator of the aerodrome
must immediately take all of the measures necessary to ensure the safety
of the aerodrome and persons at the aerodrome, including informing the
appropriate police service of the nature of the threat.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137869){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137869.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-412-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 412"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Information Reporting]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777668 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security incidents

**[[413]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-413 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must immediately notify the Minister when any of the following
incidents occur:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the discovery, at the aerodrome, of a weapon,
  explosive substance or incendiary device that is not permitted under
  subsection 78(2);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an explosion at the aerodrome, unless the explosion
  is known to be the result of an accident, excavation, demolition or
  construction work, or the use of fireworks displays;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a threat against the aerodrome; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} an aviation security incident that involves a peace
  officer anywhere at the aerodrome other than areas under an air
  carrier's control.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137872){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137872.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-413-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 413"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commercial air service information

**[[414]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-414 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must provide the Minister with written notice of any new
commercial air service that is to begin at an air terminal building.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[AVSEC Levels]{.HTitleText2} {#h-777677 .Subheading}

#### [Overview]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777678 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Division overview

**[[415]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-415 .sectionLabel}** This Division sets out
requirements respecting the implementation of additional safeguards in
the event of heightened risk conditions.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 28
:::

#### [AVSEC Level Requirements]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777683 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional safeguards

**[[416]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-416 .sectionLabel}** If the AVSEC level is
raised or maintained above level 1 for an aerodrome or any part of an
aerodrome, the operator of the aerodrome must immediately take the
following actions:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} determine which additional safeguards are likely to
  mitigate the heightened risk condition;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} notify any persons or organizations that have
  aviation security roles and responsibilities at the aerodrome and are
  affected by the heightened risk condition;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} implement or continue to implement the additional
  safeguards; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} notify the Minister of the additional safeguards that
  are being or will be implemented.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 28
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

**[[417]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-417 .sectionLabel}** When the AVSEC level is
lowered for an aerodrome or any part of an aerodrome, the operator of
the aerodrome must immediately notify the persons and organizations that
were notified under paragraph 416(b).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 28
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Legal powers and obligations

**[[418]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-418 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
nothing in these Regulations authorizes the operator of an aerodrome to
implement additional safeguards that are inconsistent with the
operator's legal powers and obligations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 28
:::

**\[419 and 420 reserved\]**

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Personnel and Training]{.HTitleText2} {#h-777701 .Subheading}

#### [Overview]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777702 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Division overview

**[[421]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-421 .sectionLabel}** This Division sets out
requirements respecting aerodrome security personnel and other persons
who are assigned aerodrome-related security roles and responsibilities
at an aerodrome.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 75
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-421-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 421"){.PITLink}
:::

**\[422 and 423 reserved\]**

#### [Security Official]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777708 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2014-153, s. 29

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

**[[424]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-424 .sectionLabel}** A security official of
an aerodrome is an individual who is responsible for

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} coordinating and overseeing security controls and
  procedures at the aerodrome; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} acting as the principal contact between the operator
  of the aerodrome and the Minister with respect to security matters,
  including the airport security program.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement

- **[[425]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-425 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must have, at all times, at least one
  security official or acting security official.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contact information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator of the aerodrome must provide the
  Minister with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of each security official and acting
    security official; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 24-hour contact information for those officials.

#### [Aerodrome Security Personnel]{.HTitleText3} {#h-777722 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Initial training

- **[[426]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-426 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must ensure that a member of the aerodrome
  security personnel does not carry out an aerodrome-related security
  role or responsibility at the aerodrome unless the member has received
  initial training in relation to that role or responsibility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Training elements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Initial training for aerodrome security personnel
  must include instruction and evaluation in relation to the topics set
  out below that are relevant to the aerodrome-related security roles
  and responsibilities of the personnel:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} international instruments respecting aviation
    security, the aviation security provisions of the Act and regulatory
    requirements;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the security controls and procedures at the
    aerodrome where the personnel are employed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} systems and equipment at the aerodrome;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an overview of threats to aviation security and
    acts or attempted acts of unlawful interference with civil aviation;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the recognition of goods that are listed or
    described in TP 14628 or that pose an immediate threat to aviation
    security; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the actions to be taken by the personnel in
    response to a threat to aviation security or an act or attempted act
    of unlawful interference with civil aviation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grandfathering

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Aerodrome security personnel who are employed at the
  aerodrome on the day on which this section comes into force are
  exempted from initial training in relation to any topic for which they
  have already received training.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 30
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Follow-up training

- **[[427]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-427 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must ensure that aerodrome security personnel
  receive follow-up training when any of the following circumstances
  arise:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a change is made in the aviation security
    provisions of the Act or in regulatory requirements and the change
    is relevant to the aerodrome-related security roles and
    responsibilities of the personnel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a change is made in the security controls and
    procedures at the aerodrome where the personnel are employed and the
    change is relevant to the aerodrome-related security roles and
    responsibilities of the personnel;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a new or modified action is to be taken by the
    personnel in response to a threat to aviation security or an act or
    attempted act of unlawful interference with civil aviation; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a significant risk or an emerging trend in aviation
    security is identified to the operator by the Minister and the risk
    or trend is relevant to the aerodrome-related security roles and
    responsibilities of the personnel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Follow-up training

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator of an aerodrome must ensure that a
  member of the aerodrome security personnel receives follow-up training
  when the Minister or the operator identifies a shortcoming in the
  member's performance when the member is carrying out security controls
  or following security procedures at the aerodrome.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Training elements

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Follow-up training must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a review of any initial-training element related to
    the circumstance set out in subsection (1) or (2) that gave rise to
    the follow-up training; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} instruction and evaluation in relation to that
    circumstance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 30
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}On-the-job training

**[[428]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-428 .sectionLabel}** If, at an aerodrome,
the initial or follow-up training of aerodrome security personnel
includes on-the-job training, the operator of the aerodrome must ensure
that the on-the-job training is provided by a person who has received
that same training or has significant experience working as a member of
the aerodrome security personnel at an aerodrome listed in Schedule 1, 2
or 3.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 30
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Training records

- **[[429]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-429 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must ensure that, for each individual who
  receives training in accordance with section 426 or 427, there is a
  training record that includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the individual's employee group or contractor
    group, if applicable, and a description of the individual's
    aerodrome-related security roles and responsibilities;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of all the training that the
    individual has received in accordance with section 426 or 427; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} evaluation results for all the training that the
    individual has received in accordance with section 426 or 427.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record keeping
