exercise that the operator plans to carry out.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 35
:::

#### [Records]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778563 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional safeguards

- **[[478]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-478 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  time additional safeguards are implemented at an aerodrome in order to
  mitigate heightened risk conditions related to aviation security, the
  operator of the aerodrome must create a record that includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the additional safeguards that
    were implemented;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an evaluation of the effectiveness of those
    additional safeguards; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of any actions that are planned in
    order to address deficiencies identified during the implementation
    of those additional safeguards.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Emergencies

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each time an emergency referred to in
  subsection 474(1) occurs at an aerodrome, the operator of the
  aerodrome must create a record that includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the emergency;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an evaluation of the effectiveness of the
    operator's emergency plan; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of any actions that are planned in
    order to address deficiencies identified during the emergency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exercises

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Each time a security exercise is carried out at an
  aerodrome, the operator of the aerodrome must create a record that
  includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an outline of the exercise scenario;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an evaluation of the effectiveness of the exercise;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of any actions that are planned in
    order to address deficiencies identified during the exercise.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 33
- SOR/2014-153, s. 35
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-478-20120315.html "Link to previous version of section 478"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Corrective Actions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778582 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corrective actions

**[[479]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-479 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 480,
the operator of an aerodrome must immediately take corrective actions to
address a vulnerability that contributes to a heightened aviation
security risk at the aerodrome and that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is identified to the operator by the Minister; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is identified by the operator.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 35
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-479-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 479"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corrective action plan

**[[480]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-480 .sectionLabel}** If a corrective action
to be taken by the operator of an aerodrome under section 479 involves a
phased approach, the operator must include in its airport security
program a corrective action plan that sets out

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the nature of the vulnerability to be addressed;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a rationale for the phased approach; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a timetable setting out when each phase of the
  corrective action plan will be completed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 35
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-480-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 480"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Primary Security Line Partners]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778596 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of information to operator of
aerodrome

- **[[481]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-481 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purpose of supporting the establishment and implementation of an
  airport security program by the operator of an aerodrome, a primary
  security line partner at the aerodrome must, on reasonable notice
  given by the operator, provide the operator with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} information respecting the measures, procedures and
    processes that the partner has in place at the aerodrome to protect
    the security of restricted areas and to prevent breaches of the
    primary security line; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a document that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} describes each area on the aerodrome's primary
      security line that is occupied by the partner,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} indicates the location of each restricted area
      access point in those areas, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} describes those restricted area access points.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of information to Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The primary security line partner must provide the
  Minister with the information and the document on reasonable notice
  given by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 35
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-481-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 481"){.PITLink}
:::

**\[482 and 483 reserved\]**

#### [Disclosure of Information]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778610 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

**[[484]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-484 .sectionLabel}** A person other than the
Minister must not disclose security-sensitive information that is
created or used under this Division unless the disclosure is required by
law or is necessary to comply or facilitate compliance with the aviation
security provisions of the Act, regulatory requirements or the
requirements of an emergency direction.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 35
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-484-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 484"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 9]{.HLabel2}[Reserved]{.HTitleText2} {#h-778615 .Subheading}

**\[485 to 494 reserved\]**

### [DIVISION 10]{.HLabel2}[Other Aerodrome Operations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-778617 .Subheading}

#### [Overview]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778618 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Division overview

**[[495]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-495 .sectionLabel}** This Division sets out
requirements respecting aerodrome operations that are not dealt with in
any other Division of this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 34
:::

#### [Construction Plans]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778623 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to notify Minister

- **[[496]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-496 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must notify the Minister of all plans to
  begin new construction or to make a change to the physical security of
  the aerodrome, if the construction or change relates to regulatory
  requirements respecting passengers, aircraft, baggage, cargo or mail.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be in writing;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} state the date on which the construction will begin
    or the change will be made; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} set out a description of the construction or change
    and the safeguards that will be implemented to maintain security in
    the areas of the aerodrome that will be affected by the construction
    activities.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 34
:::

**\[497 to 504 reserved\]**

## [PART 7]{.HLabel1}[Other Aerodromes]{.HTitleText1} {#h-778635 .Part}

### [Overview]{.HTitleText2} {#h-778636 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part overview

**[[505]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-505 .sectionLabel}** This Part sets out the
basic regulatory framework for security at aerodromes. However, this
framework is not applicable in respect of aerodromes listed in Schedule
1, 2 or 3 or in respect of any other place designated by the Minister
under subsection 6(1.1) of the [Canadian Air Transport Security
Authority Act](/eng/acts/C-11.2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-196, s. 6
- [SOR/2022-92, s. 38](#1344024-1345054){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1344024-1345054.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-505-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 505"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Authorization for Carriage of or Access to Explosive Substances and Incendiary Devices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-778641 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[506]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-506 .sectionLabel}** This Division applies
in respect of aerodromes. However, this Division does not apply in
respect of aerodromes listed in Schedule 1, 2 or 3 or in respect of any
other place designated by the Minister under subsection 6(1.1) of the
[Canadian Air Transport Security Authority Act](/eng/acts/C-11.2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-196, s. 7
- [SOR/2022-92, s. 38](#1344024-1345057){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1344024-1345057.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-506-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 506"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization

**[[507]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-507 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome may authorize a person to carry or have access to an explosive
substance or an incendiary device at the aerodrome if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the explosive substance or incendiary device is to be
  used at the aerodrome

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for excavation, demolition or construction work,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in fireworks displays,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} by persons operating explosives detection
    equipment or handling explosives detection dogs,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} by a police service, or

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} by military personnel; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operator has reasonable grounds to believe that
  the safety of the aerodrome and the safety of persons and aircraft at
  the aerodrome will not be jeopardized by the presence of the explosive
  substance or incendiary device.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Threats and Incidents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-778655 .Subheading}

#### [Application]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778656 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[508]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-508 .sectionLabel}** This Division applies
in respect of aerodromes that are not listed in Schedule 1, 2 or 3 and
that serve air carriers. This Division does not apply in respect of any
other place designated by the Minister under subsection 6(1.1) of the
[Canadian Air Transport Security Authority Act](/eng/acts/C-11.2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 36
- SOR/2015-196, s. 8
- [SOR/2022-92, s. 38](#1344024-1345060){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1344024-1345060.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-508-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 508"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Threat Response]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778661 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Area under operator's control

**[[509]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-509 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome who is made aware of a threat against an aviation facility, or
a part of the aerodrome, that is under the operator's control must
immediately determine whether there is a threat that jeopardizes the
security of the facility or that part of the aerodrome.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137876){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137876.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-509-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 509"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Area under control of other person

**[[510]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-510 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome who is made aware of a threat against an aviation facility, or
a part of the aerodrome, that is under the control of a person carrying
on any activity at the aerodrome, other than the operator, must
immediately

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} notify the person of the nature of the threat; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} determine whether there is a threat that jeopardizes
  the security of the aerodrome.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137879){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137879.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-510-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 510"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Threats

**[[511]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-511 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome who determines that there is a threat that jeopardizes the
security of the aerodrome must immediately take all of the measures
necessary to ensure the safety of the aerodrome and persons at the
aerodrome, including informing the appropriate police service of the
nature of the threat.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137883){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137883.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-511-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 511"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties of other person

**[[512]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-512 .sectionLabel}** A person, other than a
screening authority, who is carrying on any activity at an aerodrome and
who is made aware of a threat against the aerodrome must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} immediately notify the operator of the aerodrome of
  the nature of the threat; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} assist the operator of the aerodrome in determining
  whether there is a threat that jeopardizes the security of the
  aerodrome.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137886){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137886.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-512-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 512"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Threats identified by other person

**[[513]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-513 .sectionLabel}** If it is determined
under paragraph 15(b), 510(b) or 512(b) that there is a threat that
jeopardizes the security of an aerodrome, the operator of the aerodrome
must immediately take all of the measures necessary to ensure the safety
of the aerodrome and persons at the aerodrome, including informing the
appropriate police service of the nature of the threat.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137890){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137890.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-513-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 513"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Information Reporting]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778676 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security incidents

**[[514]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-514 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must immediately notify the Minister when any of the following
incidents occur:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the discovery, at the aerodrome, of a weapon,
  explosive substance or incendiary device that is not permitted under
  subsection 78(2);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an explosion at the aerodrome, unless the explosion
  is known to be the result of an accident, excavation, demolition, or
  construction work, or the use of fireworks displays;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a threat against the aerodrome; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} an aviation security incident that involves a peace
  officer anywhere at the aerodrome other than areas under an air
  carrier's control.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137894){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137894.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-514-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 514"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commercial air service information

**[[515]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-515 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must provide the Minister with written notice of any new
commercial air service that is to begin at an air terminal building.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Emergency Planning]{.HTitleText2} {#h-778685 .Subheading}

#### [Application]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778686 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[516]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-516 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  Division applies in respect of aerodromes that are not listed in
  Schedule 1, 2 or 3 and that serve

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} air carriers that conduct scheduled or
    non-scheduled services to or from an air terminal building at any of
    those aerodromes; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} air carriers that conduct scheduled international
    services from any of those aerodromes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Division does not apply in respect of any other
  place designated by the Minister under subsection 6(1.1) of the
  [Canadian Air Transport Security Authority Act](/eng/acts/C-11.2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 35
- SOR/2014-153, s. 37
- SOR/2015-196, s. 9
- [SOR/2022-92, s. 38](#1344024-1345063){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1344024-1345063.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-516-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 516"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Emergency Plans and Security Exercises]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778696 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Plan requirements

- **[[517]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-517 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  operator of an aerodrome must establish an emergency plan that sets
  out the response procedures to be followed at the aerodrome for
  coordinated responses to the following emergencies:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} bomb threats; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} hijackings of aircraft.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Response procedures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The response procedures must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} set out in detail the actions to be taken by the
    employees and contractors of the operator of the aerodrome and
    identify the responsibilities of all other persons or organizations
    involved, including, as applicable, the police, emergency response
    providers, air carriers, emergency coordination centre personnel and
    control tower or flight service station personnel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} include, if applicable, detailed procedures for the
    evacuation of air terminal buildings;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} include, if applicable, detailed procedures for the
    search of air terminal buildings;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} include detailed procedures for the handling and
    disposal of a suspected bomb; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} include detailed procedures for the detention on
    the ground of any aircraft involved in a bomb threat or hijacking.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 35
- SOR/2014-153, s. 37
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-517-20120315.html "Link to previous version of section 517"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discussion-based security exercise

**[[518]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-518 .sectionLabel}** The operator of an
aerodrome must, at least once a year, carry out a discussion-based
security exercise that tests the effectiveness of the operator's
emergency plan in response to a bomb threat or a hijacking of an
aircraft and involves the persons and organizations referred to in the
plan.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 35
- SOR/2014-153, s. 37
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-518-20120315.html "Link to previous version of section 518"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of emergencies

- **[[519]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-519 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  time an emergency referred to in subsection 517(1) occurs at an
  aerodrome, the operator of the aerodrome must create a record that
  includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the emergency;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an evaluation of the effectiveness of the
    operator's emergency plan; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of any actions that are planned in
    order to address deficiencies identified during the emergency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record of exercises

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each time a security exercise is carried out at an
  aerodrome, the operator of the aerodrome must create a record that
  includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an outline of the exercise scenario;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an evaluation of the effectiveness of the exercise;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of any actions that are planned in
    order to address deficiencies identified during the exercise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ministerial access

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The operator of an aerodrome must make the records
  referred to in subsections (1) and (2) available to the Minister on
  reasonable notice given by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2012-48, s. 35
- SOR/2014-153, s. 37
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-519-20120315.html "Link to previous version of section 519"){.PITLink}
:::

**\[520 to 524 reserved\]**

## [PART 8]{.HLabel1}[Aircraft Security]{.HTitleText1} {#h-778731 .Part}

### [Overview]{.HTitleText2} {#h-778732 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part overview

**[[525]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-525 .sectionLabel}** This Part sets out
requirements for air carriers, other operators of aircraft and persons
on board aircraft.

### [Weapons, Explosive Substances and Incendiary Devices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-778735 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Weapons

- **[[526]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-526 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  air carrier must not allow a person who is on board an aircraft to
  carry or have access to a weapon unless the air carrier has authorized
  the person to do so under section 531, 533 or 533.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Explosive substances and incendiary
  devices

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An air carrier must not allow a person who is on
  board an aircraft to carry or have access to an explosive substance or
  an incendiary device unless the air carrier has authorized the person
  to do so under section 533.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- border services officer

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply when a border
  services officer who is acting in the course of their duties carries
  or has access to defensive equipment, an agency firearm or ammunition
  on board an aircraft that is on the ground at an aerodrome.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- US preclearance officer

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply when a US
  preclearance officer who is exercising their powers or performing
  their duties or functions under the [Preclearance Act,
  2016](/eng/acts/P-19.32) carries or has access to defensive equipment,
  a US inspection agency firearm or US inspection agency ammunition on
  board an aircraft that is on the ground at an aerodrome.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- certain federal employees

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply when a person
  described in item 22, column 1, of the table to subsection 78(2) who
  is acting in the course of their duties carries or has access to
  defensive equipment, a loaded agency firearm or ammunition on board an
  aircraft that is on the ground at an aerodrome.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-268, s. 5](#1381985-1387577){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381985-1387577.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-526-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 526"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transport of loaded firearms

- **[[527]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-527 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  air carrier must not knowingly allow a person, other than a Canadian
  in-flight security officer who is acting in the course of their
  duties, to transport a loaded firearm on board an aircraft.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transport of explosive substances and
  incendiary devices

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An air carrier must not knowingly allow a person to
  transport an explosive substance, other than ammunition, or an
  incendiary device on board an aircraft unless the person notifies the
  air carrier before the explosive substance or incendiary device
  arrives at the aerodrome where it is to be accepted by the air carrier
  for transportation.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 38
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-527-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 527"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transport of unloaded firearms

**[[528]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-528 .sectionLabel}** An air carrier must not
knowingly allow a person to transport an unloaded firearm on board an
aircraft unless the person has declared to the air carrier that the
firearm is unloaded.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage of unloaded firearms

**[[529]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-529 .sectionLabel}** An air carrier that
transports an unloaded firearm on board an aircraft must store the
firearm so that it is not accessible to any person on board the aircraft
other than crew members.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- alcoholic beverages

**[[530]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-530 .sectionLabel}** An air carrier must not
provide a person who carries or has access to a firearm on board an
aircraft with any alcoholic beverage.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorizations for peace officers

**[[531]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-531 .sectionLabel}** An air carrier may
authorize a peace officer to carry or have access to an unloaded firearm
on board an aircraft if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the peace officer, while acting in the course of
  their duties, requires access to the firearm immediately before,
  during or immediately after the flight;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the peace officer informs the air carrier, at least
  two hours before the aircraft leaves the aerodrome or, in an
  emergency, as soon as feasible before the departure of the flight,
  that a firearm will be on board;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the peace officer shows a representative of the air
  carrier identification, issued by the organization employing the
  officer, that displays a photograph depicting a frontal view of the
  officer's face, the signature of the officer and the signature of an
  authorized representative of the organization employing the officer;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the peace officer completes the form used by the air
  carrier to authorize the carriage of a firearm on board an aircraft;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the air carrier verifies the identification referred
  to in paragraph (c)

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} before the peace officer enters a restricted area
    from which the officer may board the aircraft, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} before the peace officer boards the aircraft, if
    the aerodrome does not have a restricted area from which the officer
    may board the aircraft; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the air carrier provides the peace officer with the
  original or a copy of the completed form referred to in paragraph (d).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 39
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-531-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 531"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to inform

- **[[532]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-532 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  peace officer needs to carry or have access to a firearm on board an
  aircraft, the air carrier must, before the departure of the flight,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} inform the pilot-in-command of the aircraft by
    means of the original or a copy of the completed form referred to in
    paragraph 531(d); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), inform the crew members
    assigned to the flight or the aircraft and any other peace officer
    on board the aircraft.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undercover operations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a peace officer who is carrying or has access to a
  firearm on board an aircraft is engaged in an undercover operation and
  requests that the air carrier not reveal the officer's presence to any
  person on board the aircraft other than the pilot-in-command, the air
  carrier must comply with that request.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 40
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-532-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 532"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unloaded firearm authorizations --- air
carriers

- **[[533]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-533 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  air carrier may authorize the following persons to carry or have
  access to an unloaded firearm on board an aircraft if the firearm is
  necessary for survival purposes:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the pilot-in-command of the aircraft; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an employee of a federal or provincial department
    or agency who is engaged in wildlife control.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unloaded firearm authorizations ---
  other operators

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An operator of an aircraft, other than an air
  carrier, may authorize the pilot-in-command of the aircraft to carry
  or have access to an unloaded firearm and ammunition on board the
  aircraft if the firearm and ammunition are necessary for survival
  purposes.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization for certain federal
employees

**[[533.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-533.1 .sectionLabel}** An operator of an
aircraft may authorize a person described in item 22, column 1, of the
table to subsection 78(2) to carry or have access to defensive
equipment, a loaded agency firearm and ammunition on board an aircraft
if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is acting in the course of their duties;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person shows a representative of the operator of
  the aircraft a certificate of designation evidencing the designation
  described in column 1 of that table;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in advance of the flight, the operator of the
  aircraft receives from the department or agency employing the person
  information regarding the activities to be carried out on board the
  aircraft;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the operator of the aircraft verifies that the
  aircraft is equipped so that the flight can be conducted safely,
  taking into account the activities referred to in paragraph (c); and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the operator of the aircraft verifies that no other
  passengers are on board the aircraft other than

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person described in item 22, column 1, of that
    table,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an employee of a federal or provincial department
    or agency, other than a person described in item 22, column 1, of
    that table, who is acting in the course of their duties,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a police officer or police constable, or

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a person detained under the authority of a person
    described in item 22, column 1, of that table.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-268, s. 6](#1381987-1387590){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381987-1387590.html"}
:::

### [Persons in the Custody of an Escort Officer]{.HTitleText2} {#h-778782 .Subheading}

Definition of [[organization responsible for the person in
custody]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

- **[[534]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-534 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[organization responsible for the person in
  custody]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include a person or an
  organization that provides escort officer services under a contract
  for remuneration.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Air carrier conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An air carrier must not transport a person in the
  custody of an escort officer on board an aircraft unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the organization responsible for the person in
    custody has provided the air carrier with a written confirmation
    that the organization has assessed the pertinent facts and
    determined whether the person in custody is a maximum, medium or
    minimum risk to the safety of the travelling public and the
    operations of the air carrier and aerodrome;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the air carrier and the organization responsible
    for escorting the person in custody have agreed on the number of
    escort officers necessary to escort that person, which number must
    be at least

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} two escort officers to escort each person who is
      a maximum risk,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} one escort officer to escort each person who is
      a medium risk, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} one escort officer to escort not more than two
      persons who are a minimum risk;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person in custody is escorted by the agreed
    number of escort officers;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the organization responsible for the person in
    custody has given a written notice to the air carrier at least two
    hours or, in an emergency, as soon as feasible before the departure
    of the flight, stating

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the identity of each escort officer and the
      person in custody and the reasons why the person requires an
      escort,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the level of risk that the person in custody
      represents to the safety of the public, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the flight on which the person in custody will
      be transported;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} each escort officer shows a representative of the
    air carrier identification, issued by the organization responsible
    for the person in custody or the organization employing the officer,
    that displays a photograph depicting a frontal view of the officer's
    face, the signature of the officer and the signature of an
    authorized representative of the organization employing the officer;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} an escort officer completes the form used by the
    air carrier to authorize the transportation of a person in custody;
    and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the air carrier verifies the identification
    referred to in paragraph (e)

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} before the escort officer enters a restricted
      area from which the escort officer may board the aircraft, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} before the escort officer boards the aircraft,
      if the aerodrome does not have a restricted area from which the
      escort officer may board the aircraft.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Escort officer conditions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An escort officer must not escort a person in custody
  on board an aircraft unless the escort officer

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provides the operator of the aerodrome with a copy
    of the written notice referred to in paragraph (2)(d) at least two
    hours or, in an emergency, as soon as feasible before the departure
    of the flight; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shows a representative of the air carrier the
    identification referred to in paragraph (2)(e).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transport of more than one person in
  custody

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An air carrier that transports a person in custody
  who is a maximum risk to the public must not transport any other
  person in custody on board the aircraft.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Peace officer duties

- **[[535]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-535 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  escort officer who is a peace officer and who escorts a person in
  custody during a flight must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} remain with the person at all times;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} immediately before boarding the aircraft, search
    the person and the person's carry-on baggage for weapons or other
    items that could be used to jeopardize flight safety;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} search the area surrounding the aircraft seat
    assigned to the person for weapons or other items that could be used
    to jeopardize flight safety; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} carry restraining devices that can be used to
    restrain the person, if necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Air carrier duties

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an escort officer who is not a peace officer
  escorts a person in custody, the air carrier must, immediately before
  the person boards the aircraft, cause the person and the person's
  carry-on baggage to be screened for weapons or other items that could
  be used to jeopardize flight safety.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Escort officer duties

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An escort officer who is not a peace officer and who
  escorts a person in custody during a flight must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} remain with the person at all times;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that a screening of the person and the
    person's carry-on baggage for weapons or other items that could be
    used to jeopardize flight safety is carried out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} before the escort officer and the person enter a
      restricted area from which they may board the aircraft, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} before the escort officer and the person board
      the aircraft, if the aerodrome does not have a restricted area
      from which they may board the aircraft;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} search the area surrounding the aircraft seat
    assigned to the person for weapons or other items that could be used
    to jeopardize flight safety; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} carry restraining devices that can be used to
    restrain the person, if necessary.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumption of alcoholic beverages

**[[536]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-536 .sectionLabel}** A person in custody and
the escort officer who is escorting the person must not consume any
alcoholic beverage on board an aircraft.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- alcoholic beverages

**[[537]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-537 .sectionLabel}** An air carrier must not
provide a person in custody or an escort officer who is escorting the
person on board an aircraft with any alcoholic beverage.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Seating of persons in custody

**[[538]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-538 .sectionLabel}** An air carrier must not
allow a person in custody on board an aircraft to be seated next to an
exit.

### [Threat Response and Information Reporting]{.HTitleText2} {#h-778832 .Subheading}

#### [Threat Response]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778833 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Threat to aircraft --- air carriers

- **[[539]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  air carrier that is made aware of a threat against an aircraft or a
  flight must immediately determine whether there is a threat that
  jeopardizes the security of the aircraft or flight.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Threat to aircraft --- other operators

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An operator of an aircraft, other than an air
  carrier, who is made aware of a threat against an aircraft or a flight
  must immediately determine whether the threat jeopardizes the security
  of the aircraft or flight.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137897){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137897.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-539-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 539"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Threat to aircraft --- air carriers

- **[[540]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-540 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  air carrier that determines that there is a threat that jeopardizes
  the security of an aircraft or flight must immediately take all of the
  measures necessary to ensure the safety of the aircraft and the
  passengers and crew on board the aircraft, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informing the pilot-in-command, the crew members
    assigned to the aircraft or flight, the operator of the aerodrome
    and the appropriate police service of the nature of the threat;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the aircraft is on the ground, moving it to a
    place of safety at the aerodrome according to the directions of the
    operator of the aerodrome; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} inspecting the aircraft and causing a screening of
    the passengers and goods on board the aircraft to be carried out,
    unless the inspection and screening are likely to jeopardize the
    safety of the passengers and crew members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Threat to aircraft --- other operators

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An operator of an aircraft, other than an air
  carrier, who determines that there is a threat that jeopardizes the
  security of an aircraft or flight must immediately take all of the
  measures necessary to ensure the safety of the aircraft and the
  passengers and crew on board the aircraft, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} informing the pilot-in-command, the crew members
    assigned to the aircraft or flight, the operator of the aerodrome
    and the appropriate police service of the nature of the threat;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the aircraft is on the ground, moving it to a
    place of safety at the aerodrome according to the directions of the
    operator of the aerodrome; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} inspecting the aircraft and causing a search of the
    passengers and goods on board the aircraft to be carried out, unless
    the inspection and search are likely to jeopardize the safety of the
    passengers and crew members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Aircraft on ground

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the aircraft is on the ground, the
  pilot-in-command must comply with any direction given by the operator
  of the aerodrome under paragraph (1)(b) or (2)(b) or by a member of
  the appropriate police service, unless complying with the direction is
  likely to jeopardize the safety of the passengers and crew members.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137901){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137901.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Threat to facility or aerodrome --- air
carriers

- **[[541]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-541 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  air carrier that is made aware of a threat against an aviation
  facility, or a part of an aerodrome, that is under the air carrier's
  control must immediately determine whether there is a threat that
  jeopardizes the security of the facility or that part of the
  aerodrome.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Threat to facility or aerodrome ---
  other operators

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An operator of an aircraft, other than an air
  carrier, who is made aware of a threat against an aviation facility,
  or a part of an aerodrome, that is under the operator's control must
  immediately determine whether the threat jeopardizes the security of
  the facility or that part of the aerodrome.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137905){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137905.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-541-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 541"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Threat to facility or aerodrome --- air
carriers

- **[[542]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-542 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  air carrier that determines that there is a threat that jeopardizes
  the security of an aviation facility, or a part of an aerodrome, that
  is under the air carrier's control must immediately take all of the
  measures necessary to ensure the safety of the facility or that part
  of the aerodrome and the safety of persons at the facility or that
  part of the aerodrome, including informing the operator of the
  aerodrome and the appropriate police service of the nature of the
  threat.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Threat to facility or aerodrome ---
  other operators

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An operator of an aircraft, other than an air
  carrier, who determines that there is a threat that jeopardizes the
  security of an aviation facility, or a part of an aerodrome, that is
  under the operator's control must immediately take all of the measures
  necessary to ensure the safety of the facility or that part of the
  aerodrome and the safety of persons at the facility or that part of
  the aerodrome, including informing the operator of the aerodrome and
  the appropriate police service of the threat.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137909){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137909.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-542-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 542"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Reporting of Security Incidents]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778862 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of Minister

- **[[543]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-543 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  air carrier must immediately notify the Minister when any of the
  following incidents occur:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the hijacking or attempted hijacking of an
    aircraft;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the discovery, on board an aircraft, of a weapon,
    other than a weapon permitted under subsections 79(2.1) to (2.4) or
    a weapon that the air carrier authorized under section 531,
    subsection 533(1) or section 533.1;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the discovery, on board an aircraft, of an
    explosive substance or an incendiary device in respect of which the
    air carrier was not notified in accordance with subsection 80(3),
    other than ammunition permitted under subsections 79(2.1) to (2.4)
    or ammunition that the air carrier authorized under section 533.1;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an explosion on an aircraft, unless the explosion
    is known to be the result of an accident;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a threat against an aircraft, flight, or part of an
    aerodrome or other aviation facility, that is under the air
    carrier's control; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} an aviation security incident that involves a peace
    officer in any part of an aerodrome that is under the air carrier's
    control.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of operators of aerodromes

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An air carrier must immediately notify the operator
  of an aerodrome when a weapon, other than a firearm permitted under
  subsection 78(2), is detected in any part of the aerodrome that is
  under the air carrier's control.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 3](#1130155-1137912){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130155-1137912.html"}
- [SOR/2022-268, s. 7](#1381989-1387593){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1381989-1387593.html"}
:::

#### [Security Information]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778874 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision to Minister

**[[544]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-544 .sectionLabel}** An air carrier must
provide the Minister, on reasonable notice given by the Minister, with
written or electronic records or any other information relevant to the
security of the air carrier's operations, including

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} information concerning the method of implementing a
  security measure, emergency direction or interim order that applies to
  the air carrier; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the nature of the operations related
  to a particular flight and the services provided in respect of the
  flight.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty of service providers

**[[545]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-545 .sectionLabel}** A person who provides
an air carrier with a service and a person who provides a service
related to the transportation of accepted cargo or mail by air must
provide the Minister, on reasonable notice given by the Minister, with
written or electronic records or any other information relevant to the
security of the air carrier's operations, including

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} information concerning the method of implementing a
  security measure, emergency direction or interim order that applies to
  that person; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the nature of the operations related
  to a particular flight and the services provided in respect of the
  flight.

**\[546 to 616 reserved\]**

## [PART 11]{.HLabel1}[Cargo and Mail --- Flights from Canada]{.HTitleText1} {#h-778888 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Cargo]{.HTitleText2} {#h-778889 .Subheading}

#### [Application]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1526128 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[668]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-668
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Section 669 applies to air carriers
  who transport cargo on a flight departing from an aerodrome located in
  Canada.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Sections 670 to 686 apply to the following members of
  the air cargo security program who screen cargo or store, tender or
  transport secure cargo:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} regulated agents;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} certified agents; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} known consignors.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3

#### [Transporting and Tendering]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778901 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to screen cargo

- **[[669]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-669
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Cargo that is to be transported by
  an air carrier on a flight must be screened by the air carrier for
  threat items in accordance with a security measure unless the cargo
  was tendered to the air carrier for transportation by air as secure
  cargo.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An air carrier must not transport cargo on a flight
  if the cargo contains a threat item.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137553){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137553.html"}
- [SOR/2024-203, s. 2](#1490601-1526114){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1490601-1526114.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-669-20190529.html "Link to previous version of section 669"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tendering of secure cargo --- regulated
agents and certified agents

**[[670]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-670 .sectionLabel}** A regulated agent or
certified agent must not tender cargo for transportation by air as
secure cargo unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the regulated agent

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} has screened the cargo for threat items in
    accordance with a security measure,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has restricted access to the cargo in accordance
    with sections 675 to 677, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} has ensured that the cargo was not tampered with
    after it was screened for threat items; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the regulated agent or certified agent has screened
  it in order to verify that

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the cargo was screened for threat items in
    accordance with a security measure,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} access to the cargo was restricted in accordance
    with sections 675 to 677, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the cargo was not tampered with after it was
    screened for threat items.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137583){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137583.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-670-20161017.html "Link to previous version of section 670"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tendering of secure cargo --- known
consignors

**[[671]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-671 .sectionLabel}** A known consignor must
not tender cargo for transportation by air as secure cargo unless the
known consignor

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} has screened the cargo for threat items in accordance
  with a security measure;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} has restricted access to the cargo in accordance with
  sections 675 to 677; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} has ensured that the cargo was not tampered with
  after it was screened for threat items.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137589){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137589.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cargo security information

- **[[672]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-672 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  regulated agent, certified agent or known consignor must not tender
  cargo for transportation by air as secure cargo unless the cargo is
  accompanied by the information referred to in subsection (2), (3) or
  (4), as applicable, in paper or electronic format.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tendering by regulated agents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the cargo is tendered by a regulated agent
  referred to in paragraph 670(a), the information that accompanies the
  cargo must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of non-consolidated cargo

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the air waybill number or the number of a similar
      control document,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the regulated agent's name,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the regulated agent's air cargo security
      program number,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the name of the original shipper of the cargo,
      and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a declaration by one of the regulated agent's
      authorized cargo representatives stating that

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} an authorized cargo representative has screened
        the cargo for threat items in accordance with a security measure
        and no threat items were found in the cargo,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} access to the cargo was restricted in
        accordance with sections 675 to 677, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the cargo was not tampered with between the
        time it was screened for threat items and the time it was
        tendered; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of consolidated cargo

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the air waybill number or the number of a similar
      control document,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the regulated agent's name,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the regulated agent's air cargo security
      program number, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a declaration by one of the regulated agent's
      authorized cargo representatives stating that

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} an authorized cargo representative has screened
        the cargo for threat items in accordance with a security measure
        and no threat items were found in the cargo,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} access to the cargo was restricted in
        accordance with sections 675 to 677, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the cargo was not tampered with between the
        time it was screened for threat items and the time it was
        tendered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tendering by regulated agents or
  certified agents

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the cargo is tendered by a regulated agent or
  certified agent referred to in paragraph 670(b), the information that
  accompanies the cargo must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of non-consolidated cargo

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the information that accompanied the cargo when
      the cargo was accepted by the regulated agent or certified agent,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the air waybill number or the number of a
      similar control document, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the regulated agent's or certified agent's
      name,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the regulated agent's or certified agent's air
      cargo security program number, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a declaration by one of the regulated agent's or
      certified agent's authorized cargo representatives stating that

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} access to the cargo was restricted in
        accordance with sections 675 to 677, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the cargo was not tampered with between the
        time it was screened for threat items and the time it was
        tendered;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of consolidated cargo

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the air waybill number or the number of a similar
      control document,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the regulated agent's or certified agent's name,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the regulated agent's or certified agent's air
      cargo security program number, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a declaration by one of the regulated agent's or
      certified agent's authorized cargo representatives stating that

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} access to the cargo was restricted in
        accordance with sections 675 to 677, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the cargo was not tampered with between the
        time it was screened for threat items and the time it was
        tendered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tendering by known consignors

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the cargo is tendered by a known consignor
  referred to in section 671, the information that accompanies the cargo
  must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the air waybill number or the number of a similar
    control document,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the known consignor's name,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the known consignor's air cargo security program
    number, and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a declaration by one of the known consignor's
    authorized cargo representatives stating that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an authorized cargo representative has screened
      the cargo for threat items in accordance with a security measure
      and no threat items were found in the cargo,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} access to the cargo was restricted in accordance
      with sections 675 to 677, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the cargo was not tampered with between the
      time it was screened for threat items and the time it was
      tendered.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137647){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137647.html"}
:::

:::

#### [Screenings]{.HTitleText3} {#h-778973 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority to screen

**[[674]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-674 .sectionLabel}** A person must not
screen cargo unless the person is designated by the Minister or is an
authorized cargo representative of the designated person.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137654){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137654.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-674-20161017.html "Link to previous version of section 674"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Screening for threat items

- **[[675]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-675 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  regulated agent or known consignor screens cargo for threat items in
  order for it to be tendered for transportation by air as secure cargo,
  the regulated agent or known consignor must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} conduct the screening in accordance with a security
    measure;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} conduct the screening in an area that the regulated
    agent or known consignor has designated for that purpose;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} ensure that, while the screening is being
    conducted, signs are in place at or near the designated area that
    state, in both official languages, that unauthorized access to the
    area is prohibited;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} control access to the designated area while the
    screening is being conducted to ensure that, subject to subsection
    (2), only an authorized cargo representative of the regulated agent
    or known consignor has access to the area;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} take measures to detect unauthorized individuals in
    the designated area while the screening is being conducted;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} ensure that, while the screening is being
    conducted, individuals other than an authorized cargo representative
    of the regulated agent or known consignor do not have access to
    cargo in the designated area; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} ensure that, while the screening is being
    conducted, cargo in the designated area is not tampered with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized access

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An individual who is not an authorized cargo
  representative of the regulated agent or known consignor is permitted
  in the designated area if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the individual is acting in the course of their
    employment and requires access to the designated area while
    screenings are being conducted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the individual has the prior approval of the cargo
    security coordinator of the regulated agent or known consignor;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the cargo security coordinator or one of the
    regulated agent's or known consignor's authorized cargo
    representatives verifies the identity of the individual by means of
    government-issued photo identification provided by the individual;
    and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the individual is, while in the designated area,
    escorted and kept under surveillance by an authorized cargo
    representative who has been assigned to that task by the regulated
    agent or known consignor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Escort limit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (2)(d), the regulated
  agent or known consignor must ensure that the authorized cargo
  representative does not escort more than 10 individuals at one time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137675){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137675.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-675-20161017.html "Link to previous version of section 675"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Storage and Transport Requirements]{.HTitleText3} {#h-779000 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage requirements

- **[[676]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-676 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  cargo is screened in order for it to be tendered for transportation by
  air as secure cargo, a regulated agent, certified agent or known
  consignor who stores the cargo must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} store the cargo in an area that they have
    designated for that purpose;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that, while the cargo is stored in the
    designated area, signs are in place at or near the area that state,
    in both official languages, that unauthorized access to the area is
    prohibited;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} control access to the designated area while the
    cargo is stored in the area to ensure that, subject to subsection
    (2), only an authorized cargo representative of the regulated agent,
    certified agent or known consignor has access to the area;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} take measures to detect unauthorized individuals in
    the designated area while the cargo is stored in the area;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} ensure that, while the cargo is stored in the
    designated area, individuals other than an authorized cargo
    representative of the regulated agent, certified agent or known
    consignor do not have access to cargo in the designated area; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the cargo is not tampered with while it
    is stored in the designated area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized access

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An individual who is not an authorized cargo
  representative of the regulated agent, certified agent or known
  consignor is permitted in the designated area if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the individual is acting in the course of their
    employment and requires access to the designated area while cargo,
    which has been screened in order that it may be tendered for
    transportation by air as secure cargo, is stored in the area;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the individual has the prior approval of the cargo
    security coordinator of the regulated agent, certified agent or
    known consignor;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the cargo security coordinator, or one of the
    authorized cargo representatives of the regulated agent, certified
    agent or known consignor, verifies the identity of the individual by
    means of government-issued photo identification provided by the
    individual; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the individual is, while in the designated area,
    escorted and kept under surveillance by an authorized cargo
    representative who has been assigned to that task by the regulated
    agent, certified agent or known consignor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Escort limit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (2)(d), the regulated
  agent, certified agent or known consignor must ensure that the
  authorized cargo representative does not escort more than 10
  individuals at one time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137703){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137703.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-676-20161017.html "Link to previous version of section 676"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transport requirements

**[[677]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-677 .sectionLabel}** If cargo is screened in
order for it to be tendered for transportation by air as secure cargo,
the regulated agent, certified agent or known consignor who transports
the cargo must, in accordance with a security measure,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the cargo is transported by one or more
  of their authorized cargo representatives; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that there is no unauthorized access to the
  cargo during transport.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137708){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137708.html"}
:::
