[Previous
Version](section-677-20161017.html "Link to previous version of section 677"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[678]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-678 .sectionLabel}** \[Reserved,
SOR/2019-149, s. 2\]

**\[678 reserved\]**

#### [Cargo Security Coordinators and Authorized Cargo Representatives]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1137732 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cargo security coordinator ---
designation

- **[[679]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-679 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  regulated agent, certified agent or known consignor must designate a
  cargo security coordinator who is responsible for coordinating and
  overseeing compliance with the regulatory requirements that apply to
  the regulated agent, certified agent or known consignor and for acting
  as the principal contact between the regulated agent, certified agent
  or known consignor and the Minister with respect to aviation security
  matters.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cargo security coordinator ---
  restriction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The regulated agent, certified agent or known
  consignor must not designate an individual as cargo security
  coordinator unless that individual is an authorized cargo
  representative and a senior manager or supervisor.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137739){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137739.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-679-20161017.html "Link to previous version of section 679"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized cargo representative

- **[[680]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-680 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  regulated agent, certified agent or known consignor must not designate
  an individual as an authorized cargo representative unless the
  individual

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is employed by the regulated agent, certified agent
    or known consignor;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has a security clearance or has undergone a
    background check that indicates that the individual does not pose a
    risk to aviation security; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has been trained in relation to their duties as an
    authorized cargo representative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Background check

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the individual undergoes a background check for
  the purposes of paragraph (1)(b), the regulated agent, certified agent
  or known consignor must ensure that the background check includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Canadian criminal record check;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a check of the individual's home addresses for the
    five years preceding the date of the background check; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a check of the individual's work history for the
    five years preceding the date of the background check.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137751){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137751.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-680-20161017.html "Link to previous version of section 680"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Canadian criminal record check

**[[681]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-681 .sectionLabel}** A regulated agent,
certified agent or known consignor must, at least once every five years,
conduct a Canadian criminal record check on any of their authorized
cargo representatives that do not have a security clearance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137754){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137754.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-681-20161017.html "Link to previous version of section 681"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Reporting of Security Incidents]{.HTitleText3} {#h-779058 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reporting of security incidents

**[[682]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-682 .sectionLabel}** A regulated agent,
certified agent or known consignor must immediately notify the Minister
if they

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} detect a threat item in cargo while screening it in
  order for it to be tendered for transportation by air as secure cargo;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} detect unauthorized access to cargo in their
  possession that is being screened or has been screened in order that
  it may be tendered for transportation by air as secure cargo;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} detect signs of tampering with cargo in their
  possession that is being screened or has been screened in order that
  it may be tendered for transportion by air as secure cargo; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} become aware of any other aviation security incident
  involving cargo that is or was in their possession.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137770){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137770.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-682-20161017.html "Link to previous version of section 682"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Record Keeping]{.HTitleText3} {#h-779067 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cargo security information

**[[683]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-683 .sectionLabel}** A regulated agent,
certified agent or known consignor who tenders cargo for transportation
by air as secure cargo must keep a copy of the information referred to
in subsection 672(2), (3) or (4), as applicable, for at least 90 days
after the day on which they cease to be in possession of the cargo.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137774){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137774.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-683-20161017.html "Link to previous version of section 683"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Training record

- **[[684]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-684 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  regulated agent, certified agent or known consignor must keep a
  training record, in paper or electronic format, for each authorized
  cargo representative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Required information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A training record for an authorized cargo
  representative must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the authorized cargo representative;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} their position title;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the dates on which they received training in
    relation to their duties as an authorized cargo representative;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of those duties; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name of the trainer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period --- cessation of
  duties

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The regulated agent, certified agent or known
  consignor must keep the training record for at least 90 days after the
  day on which the authorized cargo representative ceases to act in that
  capacity.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137787){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137787.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-684-20161017.html "Link to previous version of section 684"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Background check record

- **[[685]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-685 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an individual undergoes a background check for the purposes of
  paragraph 680(1)(b) and is designated as an authorized cargo
  representative, the regulated agent, certified agent or known
  consignor must keep a record in respect of the background check that
  contains

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the individual;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of the background check; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person or organization that
    conducted the background check.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the individual ceases to act as an authorized
  cargo representative, the regulated agent, certified agent or known
  consignor must keep the record for at least 90 days after the day on
  which the authorized cargo representative ceases to act in that
  capacity.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137796){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137796.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-685-20161017.html "Link to previous version of section 685"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ministerial access

**[[686]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-686 .sectionLabel}** A regulated agent,
certified agent or known consignor who keeps a record or information
under this Part must make the record or information available to the
Minister on reasonable notice given by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-163, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137799){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137799.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-686-20161017.html "Link to previous version of section 686"){.PITLink}
:::

**\[687 to 719 reserved\]**

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Mail]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1137801 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to screen mail

- **[[720]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-720 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Mail
  that is to be transported by an air carrier on a flight departing from
  an aerodrome located in Canada must be screened by the air carrier for
  threat items in accordance with a security measure.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An air carrier must not transport mail on a flight
  departing from an aerodrome located in Canada if the mail contains a
  threat item.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-149, s. 2](#1130153-1137805){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1130153-1137805.html"}
- [SOR/2024-203, s. 4](#1490608-1526132){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1490608-1526132.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-720-20190529.html "Link to previous version of section 720"){.PITLink}
:::

**\[721 to 739 reserved\]**

## [PART 12]{.HLabel1}[Cargo --- Flights from Outside Canada]{.HTitleText1} {#h-779103 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[740]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-740 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies to
air carriers who transport cargo on a flight departing from a place
outside Canada to an aerodrome located in Canada. This Part does not
apply in respect of diplomatic or consular bags.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-203, s. 5](#1490615-1526144){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1490615-1526144.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cargo information

- **[[741]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-741 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  air carrier must provide to the Minister, before cargo is loaded on
  board the aircraft, the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the air waybill number;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each original shipper;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each consignee;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of each piece of cargo;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the number of pieces;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the total weight of the cargo.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change in cargo information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If there are any changes to the information, the air
  carrier must, as soon as feasible, provide the updated information to
  the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The air carrier must provide to the Minister, on
  request, any additional information respecting the cargo so that the
  aviation security risk may be assessed.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-203, s. 5](#1490615-1526159){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1490615-1526159.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cargo screening request

- **[[742]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-742 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} At
  the request of the Minister, an air carrier must screen the cargo for
  threat items in accordance with a security measure.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Threat item

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the cargo contains a threat item, the air carrier
  must immediately establish control of the cargo and must remove any
  threat to aviation security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Screening information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} After any screening carried out under subsection (1),
  the air carrier must provide the Minister with the following
  information before the flight's departure from the last point of
  departure:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the screening methods used;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date and time of the screening;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the air waybill number;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the cargo contained a threat item, the action
    taken to remove the threat.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-203, s. 5](#1490615-1526172){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1490615-1526172.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

**[[743]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-743 .sectionLabel}** An air carrier must not
transport cargo on a flight unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has confirmed receipt of the information
  referred to in subsection 741(1) and, if applicable, subsection
  741(2);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has confirmed that no further action is
  to be taken after the assessment of the additional information
  provided under subsection 741(3);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has not issued a "Do Not Load" notice in
  respect of the cargo; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has confirmed that no further action is
  to be taken after the assessment of the information provided under
  subsection 742(3).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-203, s. 5](#1490615-1526180){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1490615-1526180.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information to Minister

**[[744]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-744 .sectionLabel}** An air carrier must
provide the following information to the Minister as soon as feasible
after a flight's departure from the last point of departure and before
its arrival in Canada:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date of the flight;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the flight number;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the destination aerodrome;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the departure time;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} a list of the air waybill numbers for the flight.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-203, s. 5](#1490615-1526189){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1490615-1526189.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contact person

**[[745]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-745 .sectionLabel}** An air carrier must
provide to the Minister the name and contact information of the person
who is acting as the principal contact between the air carrier and the
Minister. The air carrier must ensure that the person is available at
all times.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-203, s. 5](#1490615-1526193){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1490615-1526193.html"}
:::

**\[746 to 764 reserved\]**

## [PART 13]{.HLabel1}[Ministerial Powers and Duties]{.HTitleText1} {#h-779105 .Part}

### [Overview]{.HTitleText2} {#h-779106 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part overview

**[[765]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-765 .sectionLabel}** This Part sets out
ministerial powers and duties that are not set out in any other part.

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Identity Verification System]{.HTitleText2} {#h-779109 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2014-153, s. 41

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of information

**[[766]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-766 .sectionLabel}** The Minister is
authorized to disclose to CATSA or the operator of an aerodrome any
information that is necessary for the proper operation of the identity
verification system referred to in section 56.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deactivation request

- **[[767]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-767 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must ask CATSA to deactivate a restricted area identity card
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister is notified under section 156 or 312;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the security clearance of the person to whom the
    card has been issued is suspended or cancelled.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must not ask CATSA to deactivate a
  restricted area identity card for a reason other than a reason set out
  in subsection (1).

**\[768 to 777 reserved\]**

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[AVSEC Levels]{.HTitleText2} {#h-779122 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[778]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-778 .sectionLabel}** This Division applies
in respect of aerodromes listed in Schedules 1 to 3, or any part of
those aerodromes, and in respect of any other place designated by the
Minister under subsection 6(1.1) of the [Canadian Air Transport Security
Authority Act](/eng/acts/C-11.2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 42
- SOR/2015-196, s. 10
- [SOR/2022-92, s. 38](#1344024-1345066){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1344024-1345066.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-778-20150716.html "Link to previous version of section 778"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Level 1

**[[779]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-779 .sectionLabel}** Unless it is raised,
lowered or maintained in accordance with this Division, the AVSEC level
for an aerodrome or any part of an aerodrome is level 1. At that level,
normal operating conditions apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Level 2

**[[780]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-780 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must raise
or lower to level 2 the AVSEC level for an aerodrome or any part of an
aerodrome if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister is made aware of a heightened risk
  condition related to an elevated risk; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is likely, based on available information, that
  additional safeguards at the aerodrome or a part of the aerodrome will
  mitigate the heightened risk condition.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Level 3

**[[781]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-781 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must raise
to level 3 the AVSEC level for an aerodrome or any part of an aerodrome
if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister is made aware of a heightened risk
  condition related to a critical or imminent risk; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is likely, based on available information, that
  additional safeguards at the aerodrome or a part of the aerodrome will
  mitigate the heightened risk condition.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to lower level

**[[782]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-782 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must, as
soon as a heightened risk condition no longer applies, lower to level 1
an AVSEC level that has been raised for an aerodrome or any part of an
aerodrome.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maintaining a level

**[[783]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-783 .sectionLabel}** The Minister is
authorized to maintain an AVSEC level that has been raised for an
aerodrome or any part of an aerodrome if the criteria for raising the
AVSEC level continue to apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

**[[784]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-784 .sectionLabel}** If the Minister raises,
lowers or maintains the AVSEC level for an aerodrome or any part of an
aerodrome, the Minister must immediately notify the operator of the
aerodrome. The notice must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} include information about the heightened risk
  condition; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} specify a date on which the AVSEC level is likely to
  return to level 1.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 42
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Multiple aerodromes

**[[785]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-785 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
nothing in this Division prohibits the Minister from raising, lowering
or maintaining the AVSEC level for more than one aerodrome at a time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 42
:::

**\[786 to 796 reserved\]**

## [PART 14]{.HLabel1}[Designated Provisions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-779162 .Part}

### [Overview]{.HTitleText2} {#h-779163 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part overview

- **[[797]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-797 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  Part allows the enforcement, by means of administrative monetary
  penalties, of the provisions of these Regulations that are set out in
  Schedule 4 and the provisions of any security measure.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designated provisions of the Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The [Designated Provisions
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2000-112) allow the enforcement, by
  means of administrative monetary penalties, of the provisions of the
  Act that are set out in Schedule 4 to those Regulations.

### [Designated Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-779169 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designated provisions

- **[[798]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-798 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  provision set out in column 1 of Schedule 4 is designated as a
  provision the contravention of which may be dealt with under and in
  accordance with the procedure set out in sections 7.7 to 8.2 of the
  Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum amounts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount set out in column 2 or column 3 of
  Schedule 4 is prescribed as the maximum amount payable by an
  individual or corporation, as the case may be, in respect of a
  contravention of the provision set out in column 1.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation of security measure
provisions

- **[[799]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-799 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  provisions of a security measure are designated as provisions the
  contravention of which may be dealt with under and in accordance with
  the procedure set out in sections 7.7 to 8.2 of the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum amounts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount payable in respect of a
  contravention of a designated provision referred to in subsection (1)
  is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$5,000, in the case of an individual; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} \$25,000, in the case of a corporation.

### [Notice of Contravention]{.HTitleText2} {#h-779182 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice requirements

**[[800]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-800 .sectionLabel}** A notice referred to in
subsection 7.7(1) of the Act must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be in writing;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} set out the particulars of the alleged contravention;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} state that the person on whom the notice is served or
  to whom it is sent has the option of paying the amount specified in
  the notice or filing with the Tribunal a request for a review of the
  alleged contravention or the amount of the penalty;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} state that payment of the amount specified in the
  notice will be accepted by the Minister in satisfaction of the amount
  of the penalty for the alleged contravention and that no further
  proceedings under Part I of the Act will be taken against the person
  on whom the notice in respect of that contravention is served or to
  whom it is sent;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} state that the person on whom the notice is served or
  to whom it is sent will be provided with an opportunity consistent
  with procedural fairness and natural justice to present evidence
  before the Tribunal and make representations in relation to the
  alleged contravention if the person files a request for a review with
  the Tribunal; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} state that the person on whom the notice is served or
  to whom it is sent will be deemed to have committed the contravention
  set out in the notice if the person fails to pay the amount specified
  in the notice and fails to file a request for a review with the
  Tribunal within the prescribed period.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-153, s. 43
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-800-20120101.html "Link to previous version of section 800"){.PITLink}
:::

  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Sections 196 and 199 do not apply to
